lock_path = WindowsPath('C:/Users/郑曾波/Projects/invest-quick-scan/runs/remaining-final-review-2026-10-18/isolated/inputs.lock.json')
expected_sha = None
inputs_root = WindowsPath('C:/Users/郑曾波/Projects/invest-quick-scan/runs/remaining-final-review-2026-10-18/isolated/review-inputs')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):

                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():

                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue

                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')

                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')

                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:

            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')

                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)

            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')

expected_sha = None
inputs_root = WindowsPath('C:/Users/郑曾波/Projects/invest-quick-scan/runs/remaining-final-review-2026-10-18/isolated/review-inputs')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:

                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)

            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):

expected_sha = None
inputs_root = WindowsPath('C:/Users/郑曾波/Projects/invest-quick-scan/runs/remaining-final-review-2026-10-18/isolated/review-inputs')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc
        protocol = lock.get('protocol') if isinstance(lock, dict) else None
        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
        for row in lock['files']:
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock row must be an object')
            name = row.get('path')
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not name or '\\' in name or ':' in name:
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock relative path')
            rel = PurePosixPath(name)
            if rel.is_absolute() or any(p in ('', '.', '..') for p in name.split('/')):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path escape refused')
            if name.casefold() in seen:
                raise CalibrationError(f'duplicate input lock path: {name}')
            seen.add(name.casefold())
            consumers = row.get('consumers')
            if not isinstance(consumers, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in consumers):
                raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock consumers')
            if 'REVIEW-CAL-06' not in consumers:
                continue
            path = inputs_root.joinpath(*rel.parts); reject_links(path)
            if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(root):
                raise CalibrationError('input lock path outside inputs root')
            size = row.get('bytes'); sha = row.get('raw_sha256')
            if isinstance(size, bool) or not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or not isinstance(sha, str) or len(sha) != 64 or any(c not in '0123456789abcdef' for c in sha):
                raise CalibrationError(f'invalid input lock metadata: {name}')
            if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size != size or hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
                raise CalibrationError(f'consumer input {name} does not match locked raw bytes')
            verified[name] = row
        if not verified:
            raise CalibrationError('empty REVIEW-CAL-06 consumer subset')
        # No extra Python module may enter the frozen import search roots.
        for base in ('frozen/evaluation/src', 'frozen/iqs/scripts', 'evaluation/src', 'iqs/scripts'):
            directory = inputs_root/base
            if directory.exists():
                for path in directory.rglob('*'):
                    if path.suffix.lower() not in ('.py', '.pyc', '.pyo', '.pyd'):
                        continue
                    reject_links(path)
                    name = path.relative_to(inputs_root).as_posix()
                    if name not in verified:
>                       raise CalibrationError(f'executable consumer is not locked: {name}')

    def verify_consumer(lock_path: Path, expected_sha: str | None, inputs_root: Path) -> dict:
        reject_links(lock_path); reject_links(inputs_root)
        if lock_path.stat().st_size > 8 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise CalibrationError('input lock too large')
        raw = lock_path.read_bytes(); digest = hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()
        if expected_sha is not None and digest != expected_sha:
            raise CalibrationError(f'lock hash mismatch: {lock_path} hashes to {digest} but {expected_sha} was expected')
        try:
            lock = json.loads(raw.decode('utf-8'), object_pairs_hook=_pairs,
                              parse_float=_float, parse_constant=_constant)
        except (ValueError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
            raise CalibrationError('invalid input lock JSON') from exc

        if isinstance(protocol, str) and protocol.startswith(LOCK_PROTOCOL.rsplit('/',1)[0]+'/'):
            major = protocol.rsplit('/',1)[1].split('.',1)[0]
            if major.isdigit() and major != '1':
                raise UnsupportedVersionError(f'unsupported input lock major version: {major}')
        if not isinstance(lock, dict) or protocol != LOCK_PROTOCOL or not isinstance(lock.get('files'), list):
            raise CalibrationError('unknown or invalid input lock protocol/files')
        root = inputs_root.resolve(); seen = set(); verified = {}
